- Consequences: 77개 인코딩 슬러그 전원을 각각 유일한 Burst 3로 고정 지원 셸에 넣고 측정한 결과 **75개의 딜이 변했다 — 전부 상승**(옛 동작은 살아있어야 할 버프를 지우는 방향으로만 틀릴 수 있었으므로). Liberalio +132.6%, ark-ranger-black +44.0%, snow-white-heavy-arms +33.6%, bready-recommended +28.6%. 넓게 퍼진 +4~17% 꼬리는 대부분 **같은 날 추가된 Crown의 Royal Attire**(refreshing 스쿼드 공격피해, 아래 결정 참고)가 그녀 자신의 Last Kingdom 버스트 버프(+36.24%)를 매번 지우던 회귀 때문이었다(0.5723 = 0.3624 + 0.2099로 공존 확인). 앞으로 새 refreshing 버프를 추가할 때는 **그 유닛이 같은 (stat, scope)를 다른 불릿에서도 주는지** 먼저 확인해야 한다는 교훈을 `docs/insights.md`에도 기록.
- **Update 2026-08-13 — `refresh_group`이 `truncate_open_ended`에도 옵션 인자로 확장됐다.** 퀸(마코토 니지마)이 처음으로 한 유닛이 같은 스탯에 계속형 버프를 둘(영구 Nuke Boost + 풀버스트 종료까지인 Nuke Amp, 둘 다 Elemental Advantage Attack Damage) 들고 나중 트리거가 그중 하나만 끝내는 형태를 냈다 — 기존 `truncate_open_ended`는 `(stat, source_slug)`가 같은 열린 효과를 전부 닫아 영구 쪽까지 죽였을 것이다. `add_refreshing`이 같은 이유로 이미 요구하는 `refresh_group`을 선택 인자로 받게 확장해 그 불릿만 닫는다 — 인자를 안 주면 기존 동작(전부 닫힘) 그대로라 그레이브·아르카나는 불변. `backend/app/effects.py::EffectRegistry.truncate_open_ended`.

## 차지속도 공식 교체: `÷(1+s)` → `×(1−s)`, 프레임 단위 내림, 측정 기반 하한

> **하한 부분만 폐기 (2026-08-15).** 공식(`×(1−s)`)과 프레임 내림은 그대로 유효하다.
> 틀린 것은 **하한의 정체**다 — 여기 적힌 「메커니즘은 추정」이라는 캐비엇이 맞았고,
> 그 값 10/29 = 0.3448초는 신데렐라 무기의 **180발/분**(0.33333초 = 정확히 30발,
> 정수 20프레임)을 「29~30발」의 아래쪽 끝으로 읽은 것이었다. 전역 상수는 삭제되고
> 유닛별 `CHARGE_ROUNDS_PER_MINUTE`로 갔다 — 맨 위 「차지 무기의 `rate_of_fire`는
> 바닥값으로 받는다」 참고.

- Date: 2026-07-20 ~ 2026-07-21
- Context: `attack_rate.py`의 차지속도 모델은 재장전속도처럼 `charge_time / (1 + speed)`였다. Cinderella: Crystal Wave의 Flawless Glass(자기 차지속도 +100%)를 인코딩하는 과정에서, 이 공식은 10초에 20발을 예측하는데 Fienn의 실측(60fps, 최대탄약 오버로드로 재장전 배제)은 **29~30발/10초**였다.
- Alternatives considered: (a) 공식은 그대로 두고 Cinderella 한 명만 측정된 케이던스로 역산한 수치를 손으로 건넨다 — 이 수정 이전엔 실제로 이렇게 하고 있었다. 근본 공식이 틀려 있으므로 다음 소비자마다 같은 역산을 반복해야 하고 원인을 안 고친다. (b) `×(1−s)`로 교체하되 연속값을 그대로 쓴다(하한 없음) — +100% 이상에서 차지시간이 0 이하가 되어 발사가 무한해지고, Cinderella의 실측 상한(29~30발/10초)을 설명할 수 없다. (c) `×(1−s)` + 프레임(1/60초) 단위 내림 + 측정 기반 하한. 채택.
- Decision: (c). `charge_time_with_speed(charge_time, speed)`로 다섯 호출부를 중앙화. `frames = int(charge_time / (1/60) * speed)`(0 방향 절삭), `reduced = charge_time - frames × (1/60)`, `floor = min(charge_time, CHARGE_INTERVAL_FLOOR_SECONDS)`(`= 10.0/29`), 결과는 `max(reduced, floor)`.
- Why:
  - **`÷(1+s)` 배제 근거**: Cinderella +100% 기준 예측 20발 vs 실측 29~30발 — 반비례 공식은 버프가 아무리 커도 발사 간격이 절반에 수렴할 뿐 0에 도달하지 못해 이 실측을 재현할 수 없다.
  - **프레임 단위 내림 채택 근거**: 연속 `×(1−s)`만으로는 부족하다는 것이 두 번째 실측(Neon: Vision Eye, 차지속도 오버로드 9.47%, 기본 차지 1.0초)에서 드러났다 — 실측 0.9178초를 연속값은 0.75프레임 어긋나게 재현하는 반면, 5프레임 단위로 내린 값은 **0.07프레임**까지 맞는다. 커뮤니티가 말하는 검증 규칙("3초는 180프레임, 10.28%의 180프레임 ≈ 18.5프레임")과도 일치하고, 일부 유닛 가이드가 "99%+ 차지속도"를 권장하는 이유도 설명된다 — 100%가 차지시간을 완전히 소거하는 지점이라 나눗셈 공식으로는 애초에 표현이 불가능했다.
  - **하한 채택 근거와 한계**: Cinderella의 실측 1점(29~30발/10초 → 발당 0.3448초)에만 앵커되어 있다. **메커니즘은 추정**이다 — "샷 사이 최소 간격"과 "차지속도 버프 자체에 걸리는 캡"이 이 한 점만으로는 구분 불가능하고, 값의 출처가 로켓런처(RL) 한 종류뿐이라 SR 등 다른 무기군에서 같은 값인지도 미검증. 하한식이 무조건 `max(reduced, FLOOR)`가 아니라 `min(기본, FLOOR)`를 상한으로 잡는 이유: 이 결정 시점 데이터상 Scarlet: Black Shadow의 기본 차지시간은 0.3초로, 이 하한(0.3448초)보다 이미 빠르다. 무조건 `max(reduced, FLOOR)`를 적용하면 **버프가 하나도 없는 유닛조차 하한까지 느려지는 회귀**가 생긴다 — `min(기본, FLOOR)`로 "자기 기본 차지시간보다 느려지지 않는다"를 보장한다(이후 Scarlet의 기본 차지시간이 실측으로 0.7323초로 정정되며 이 사례는 하한보다 한참 위로 이동했다 — 아래 "무기 차지시간 실측 교정" 결정 참고).
- Consequences: E2E(180초): cinderella +11.8%(자기 +100%, 24발 탄창), red-hood +0.9%, bready-lingering +0.6%(자기 디버프라 완화 방향), scarlet·neon·maxwell·raven·ein·helm·liberalio는 0.0%(차지속도 버프를 아예 안 받음) — 실질 영향 범위는 좁다(로스터에서 차지속도를 부여하는 유닛이 6명뿐이고 그중 5명은 자기 자신에게만). 하한 메커니즘의 남은 불확실성은 곧이어 "캐스터 기준 차지 버프" 결정에서 부분적으로 해소됨(아래).

## 캐스터 기준(시전자 기준) 차지 버프는 별개 메커니즘 — 신규 `charge_time_reduction_sec` 스탯
- Date: 2026-07-21
- Context: 위 프레임 양자화 작업 직후, "차지속도 n%"가 항상 **수신자 자신의** 기본 차지시간에 대한 비율인지 검증하는 과정에서 한 차례 결론을 되돌렸다 — 그때까지 근거로 쓴 두 유닛(Neon 9.47%, Cinderella 100%)이 공교롭게 **둘 다 기본 차지 1.0초**라, `기본×(1−s)`와 `기본−s초`(절대 초 차감)가 이 데이터로는 수치적으로 구분 불가능했다. 이 상태로는 Liberalio: Calm Depths("스킬 시전자 Charge Speed의 12.74%")와 Mana: Metal sigma(−0.18초 flat, 이미 오래 defer 중이던 항목 — "비딜 스탯"이라는 옛 근거는 Phase S로 만료됐지만 "flat 초 vs 엔진의 배율 스탯 불일치"라는 새 근거로 계속 막혀 있었음)를 어떤 값으로 인코딩할지 답이 없었다.
- Alternatives considered: (a) 두 스킬 다 퍼센트로 강행 인코딩 — 등가 퍼센트가 수혜자의 기본 차지시간에 따라 달라진다는 게 드러나(Scarlet 0.73초 기준 26.1% vs 1.0초 유닛 기준 19.1%) 어느 수혜자에게 어떤 값을 줘야 할지 답이 없다. (b) 둘 다 계속 defer. (c) "시전자 기준"이라는 스킬 문구를 힌트로 보고, 이 부류의 버프를 수신자 기준 %가 아니라 **캐스터 자신의 차지시간에 곱해 절대 초로 건네는, 아예 다른 메커니즘**이라고 가설화 — Liberalio는 SR로 기본 차지 1.5초이므로 12.74%×1.5 = 0.1911초. 이 값을 한국 커뮤니티 서술("약 0.19초 줄어든다")과 Fienn의 Scarlet 실측(0.7323→0.5424초, 오차 0.07프레임)으로 이중 확인. 채택.
- Decision: (c). 신규 스탯 `charge_time_reduction_sec`을 `attack_rate`에 `charge_speed_percent` 다음 단계로 배선(퍼센트 적용 후 차감). 소비자 둘: **Liberalio Calm Depths** — 덱 내 최저 최종ATK Burst 3 아군에게 부여, 신규 `SquadContext.lowest_atk_slugs`는 캐스터 자신을 후보 풀에서 **제외하지 않는다**(한국 가이드가 "Liberalio 공격력이 대상보다 높아야 한다"고 프레이밍하는 건 그녀가 풀에 있다는 뜻이고, 자기 버프를 자기가 먹는 게 가이드가 경고하는 편성 실수). 그녀의 차지속도 면역(별도 결정, 아래)도 `charge_speed_percent`뿐 아니라 이 스탯까지 함께 막도록 확장 — 스킬이 면역을 부여하는 대상은 엔진 필드가 아니라 "차지속도"라는 개념이므로. **Mana Metal sigma** −0.18초 — 덱 내 **기본** 차지시간이 가장 긴 아군에게(신규 `longest_charge_time_slugs`, SquadContext가 이제 기본 차지시간을 들고 있음). Mana의 모듈 docstring이 이 형태를 미리 예언했다: "flat 0.18초짜리 두 번째 소비자가 생기면 그때 이 스탯을 만들라" — 정확히 그 상황이 발생해 만들었다.
- Why: 등가 퍼센트가 수신자마다 달라진다는 사실 자체가 퍼센트 인코딩이 틀렸다는 증거다. "캐스터 기준"이라는 스킬 문구가 실제로 별개 메커니즘을 가리킨다는 걸 텍스트+실측 이중 확인으로 검증했으므로, 앞서 되돌렸던 불확실성("기본×(1−s)" vs "기본−s초")은 애초에 하나의 질문이 아니라 **서로 다른 두 메커니즘을 섞어 본 것**이었다고 정리된다: 일반 `charge_speed_percent`(오버로드·큐브 등 대부분)는 수신자 자신의 기본 차지시간에 대한 %(프레임 양자화, 위 결정), "시전자 기준"이라 명시된 스킬만 캐스터 기준 절대 초.
- Consequences: E2E(180초, Scarlet+Liberalio, Liberalio가 Scarlet보다 ATK 높게 세팅): Scarlet 발수 214→285(+33.2%), 자체딜 +23.0%, 덱 총딜 +13.0%. **잔여 불확실성**: 일반 `charge_speed_percent`가 정말 "수신자 자신의 기본 차지시간에 대한 %"인지를, 기본 차지시간이 1.0초가 아닌 유닛에 일반 오버로드를 얹어 직접 판별한 적은 없다(초기에 제안됐던 Raven 대상 판별 실험은 실행되지 않았다) — 이번에 확인된 건 "캐스터 기준" 부류가 다른 메커니즘이라는 것이고, 그 발견이 정황상 일반 부류의 기존 해석을 뒷받침할 뿐이다. Liberalio의 "12.74%"를 둘러싼 예전의 모호함(19.0%냐 25.9%냐)도 이걸로 해소 — 애초에 어느 쪽도 아니었고 flat 0.1911초였다.

## 무기 차지시간을 실측으로 교정 (Scarlet, Raven) — 계통 오류가 아니라 개별 오류
- Date: 2026-07-20 ~ 2026-07-21
- Context: 차지속도 공식 수정 작업 도중, Fienn이 Scarlet: Black Shadow의 풀버스트 창을 60fps로 프레임 단위 실측했다(차지속도 버프·오버로드 없음): 14타/9.52초, 균등 간격 → 발당 0.732초. 수집 데이터(lootandwaifus)는 그녀의 기본 차지시간을 0.30초로 기록하고 있었고, 엔진은 그 값으로 같은 창에서 33발을 쐈다 — 2.4배 빠름. Scarlet의 딜은 대부분 풀차지마다 걷는 3/6/9단 시퀀스(Fleetly Fading)라, 발수 오류가 그녀의 가장 큰 딜 소스를 그대로 곱해 특히 크게 튄다.
- Alternatives considered: (a) 데이터 파일(`data/lootandwaifus/char_*.json`)의 필드를 직접 고친다 — `data/`는 이 프로젝트에서 gitignore 대상이라 고쳐도 커밋되지 않고 유실된다(워크트리에서 특히 위험). (b) 기존 `_WEAPON_PROFILE_OVERRIDE_BUILDERS` 훅(Cinderella: Crystal Wave의 스나이프 모드 전환에 이미 쓰이던 것)을 확장해, 빌더가 수집된 프로필을 인자로 받아 원하는 필드 하나(`charge_time`)만 교정하고 나머지는 그대로 통과시키게 한다. 채택. (c) 이 오류가 계통적(수집기 자체가 차지시간을 잘못 뽑는다)일 가능성이 있으므로, 로스터의 다른 차지무기 유닛도 표본 재실측 없이 하나만 고치고 끝내지 않는다.
- Decision: (b) + (c). Scarlet 0.30초 → **0.7323초**로 교정. 이어서 계통 오류 여부를 판별하기 위해 기본 차지 1.0초로 기록된 다른 로켓런처(RL) 두 명을 같은 방법(재장전속도 100% 초과 버프로 재장전 배제, 60fps 프레임 실측)으로 측정: **Raven**(차지속도 오버로드 0%) 5타/8.11초 = 발당 2.03초 → 수집값 1.0초와 불일치, 교정. **Neon: Vision Eye**(차지속도 오버로드 9.47%) 발당 0.918초 → 역산한 기본 ≈1.0초로 **수집값과 일치, 교정 불필요**.
- Why: Neon이 계통 오류 가설을 배제하는 결정적 증거다 — 29개 차지무기 유닛 전체가 아니라, **검을 쓰는 "로켓런처" 유닛 두 명(Scarlet, Raven)에 한정된 개별 오류**로 좁혀진다. 커뮤니티 가이드도 정성적으로 같은 결론을 뒷받침한다: nikke.gg는 Scarlet이 표기 0.3초에도 불구하고 "auto로 두면 ~0.7초마다 1발, charge delay를 겪는다"고 설명하고, prydwen.gg는 Raven을 "느린 아이 — 2초 재장전, 애니메이션 락"으로 묘사한다. 즉 실제 게임에서는 차지 자체가 아니라 검을 휘두르는 애니메이션이 케이던스를 결정하는데, 수집 데이터는 무기 스펙상의 "차지시간"만 담고 있어 이 두 캐릭터에서만 어긋난다.
- Consequences: E2E(180초): **Scarlet** 자체딜 6,404M → 2,930M(**−54.2%**), 덱 총딜 −45.3% — 시퀀스 트리거형 딜이라 발수 오류가 그대로 딜에 전이된 사례. **Raven** 발수 141→78(−45%)인데 딜은 1,567M → 1,397M(**−10.9%**)뿐 — 구조가 다르면 같은 종류의 교정이 정반대 크기로 나타난다: Shock Wave가 캡 10에서 포화하는 스택형 지속딜(DoT)이고 그 5초 카운터 수명이 2.03초 간격보다 길어서, 케이던스가 느려져도 고점(포화 딜)은 거의 안 내려간다(1,278M→1,215M). Scarlet의 기본 차지시간이 위 하한(0.3448초)보다 한참 위로 올라오면서, "그녀가 하한 때문에 더 빨라질 수 없다"는 이전의 미결 우려도 함께 해소됐다.

## Liberalio의 차지속도 면역 — 신규 레지스트리 개념 `set_external_stat_immunity`
- Date: 2026-07-20
- Context: Strange Currents가 "증가/감소 차지속도 효과에 대한 영구 면역(제거 불가)"을 명시한다. Phase S 이전에는 차지속도가 딜에 아무 영향이 없어 무시해도 무방했지만, Phase S(차지속도가 실제 발사 간격을 결정하도록 배선된 시점)부터는 이걸 빼놓으면 **차지속도 버퍼가 있는 덱에서 엔진이 그녀를 인게임에선 불가능한 속도로 쏘게 만드는 과대평가**가 된다 — 그런 버퍼가 있는 덱에서만 나타나 아무도 눈치채기 어려운 종류의 오류.
- Alternatives considered: (a) 그녀에게 도달하는 차지속도 버프를 출처별로 걸러내는 조건을 스킬 규칙 안에 개별 하드코딩 — 새 차지속도 버퍼가 로스터에 추가될 때마다 그녀의 코드를 다시 손봐야 한다. (b) 레지스트리에 "이 (유닛, 스탯) 조합은 자기 자신 외의 출처를 전부 버린다"는 일반 개념을 신설. 채택.
- Decision: (b). `EffectRegistry.set_external_stat_immunity(slug, stat)` — 해당 (슬러그, 스탯)에 대해, 그 유닛 자신이 아닌 출처의 효과를 `total_for` 조회에서 걸러낸다. 이 형태이기 때문에 Fienn의 조건(자기 오버로드·큐브는 받되 덱 버프만 면역)이 자연히 따라온다 — 오버로드 롤과 큐브는 `roster._passive_effects`가 **유닛 자신의 슬러그를 출처로** 등록하므로 면역을 통과하고, 아군 스킬의 소스는 그 아군의 슬러그이므로 걸러진다.
- Why: "자기 출처는 통과, 남의 출처만 거부"라는 요구사항이 정확히 게임 내 "면역"의 의미이고, 이를 유닛별 하드코딩이 아니라 레지스트리 개념 하나로 표현하면 다음에 같은 유형의 면역(다른 유닛, 다른 스탯)이 필요할 때도 재사용된다.
- Consequences: 기존 유닛 중 이 면역을 켜는 것이 없어 다른 모든 타임라인은 불변(전체 스위트 통과 확인). 같은 커밋에서 Calm Depths의 "차지속도는 inert라 보류"라는 예전 사유도 정정 — Phase S로 만료된 근거였고, 진짜 막던 건 최저ATK 랭킹 부재와 모호한 수치("12.74%")였다(위 "캐스터 기준 차지 버프" 결정에서 해소).

## Crown Royal Attire의 힐 트리거 모델 — 엔진에 힐 이벤트가 없어 floor/ceiling 두 경로로 근사
- Date: 2026-07-20
- Context: Crown의 두 번째 스킬 Royal Attire는 원래 인코딩 때부터 "노멀공격 카운터에 게이팅되는데, 그걸 표현할 발사-속도 모델이 없다"는 이유로 보류돼 있었다. `attack_rate.py`와 `per_shot_rules`가 2026-07-11에 들어오면서 그 전제는 만료됐다. 체인 전체(Relax 스택 → 최대스택에서 자힐+무적+도발) 중 딜에 관련된 건 마지막 링크뿐이다: 회복이 발동하면(누구의 회복이든) 스쿼드 전체에 공격피해 +20.99%/7초.
- Alternatives considered: (a) Crown 자신의 회복만 반영(43×20=860발마다 1회) — 트리거 원문이 "어떤 아군의 회복이든" 반응한다는 걸 무시하는 것이라, 힐러가 여럿인 덱에서는 낙관이 아니라 **비관** 방향으로 틀린다(과소평가). (b) 엔진에 진짜 힐 이벤트를 신설해 모든 힐 스킬의 실제 타이밍을 시뮬레이션 — 엔진에 없는 축(회복량/타이밍)을 새로 만드는 대규모 확장이라 이 한 스킬만을 위해서는 과하다. (c) 힐 이벤트가 없다는 것을 받아들이고, 두 경로로 나눠 모델링한다: 자기 860발 체인을 **floor**로, 덱에 다른 힐러가 있으면 그 창이 유지된다고 보는 **ceiling**으로. 채택.
- Decision: (c). 자기 체인은 `per_shot_rules("every", 860)`로 인코딩(MG 케이던스 기준 약 21.5초 소요, 180초 전투에 약 8회 발동, 가동률 ~31%) — 그녀가 덱의 유일한 힐러일 때 이 경로만 적용된다. 다른 힐러가 있으면 7초 창이 "계속 유지된다"고 근사(전투 시작 시 상시 버프로 등록). 누가 "힐러"인지는 손으로 목록을 적지 않고 `scripts/find_heal_providers.py`가 수집된 스킬 원문에서 유도한다(77명 중 16명; 커버 체력 회복은 회복받는 유닛이 없으므로 제외 — Liter·Zwei가 이 필터로 빠짐). Crown 자신도 이 명단에 있으므로(자기 체인 끝에 자힐이 있음), 신규 조건 `deck_contains_any`가 **캐스터를 제외**하도록 만들어 그녀가 자기 자신을 트리거해 ceiling 경로가 항상 켜지는 것을 막았다.
- Why: **ceiling을 택한 것은 이 프로젝트의 기존 floor 선호 관례에서 벗어난 선택**이므로 근거를 명시할 필요가 있다 — Fienn 판단(2026-07-20): 자주 회복시키는 힐러(예: Helm은 풀차지마다 회복)가 있는 덱에서는 7초 창이 실제로 거의 끊기지 않고 유지되는 게 실제 플레이 방식과 일치하고, 드물게 회복하는 힐러라면 애초에 이 조건을 만족하는 덱으로 잘 안 쓰인다. 자기 체인만 반영하는 (a)는 오히려 더 흔한 실제 플레이(다른 힐러와 함께 쓰는 덱)를 과소평가하게 된다.
- Consequences: E2E(180초, Helm이 있는 덱): 덱 총딜 +1.5% — 작은 이유는 `damage_formula`에서 모든 `*_up` 버킷이 하나의 가산 풀을 공유하기 때문에(이 덱의 기저 damage_up이 이미 ~14.9라 +21%가 크게 희석됨), +0.21→+1.41%, +1.0→+6.70%로 선형성 확인됨. `scripts/find_heal_providers.py`는 재사용 가능한 스크립트로 남겨, 새 유닛을 인코딩할 때마다 재실행해 힐러 명단을 갱신해야 한다.

## `core_level` removed from `UserNikkeState` and the roster form — same reason as `pve_cube`
- Date: 2026-07-20
- Context: `core_level` was a required field on `UserNikkeState` and a live number input on every roster card, but the backend never read it anywhere — its value is already folded into the imported level-400 `raid400` ATK/HP, the same way the harmony cube's flat stats are already folded in rather than added back on top (see the harmony-cube entry below). An earlier task in this plan added `InvestmentBadge` (grade/core, read-only, sourced from the collector's `roster.json`) to show the user's real breakthrough/core investment; leaving the inert `core_level` input beside a badge that already displays the real number was worse than either alone — it invited the user to hand-edit a value that changes nothing.
- Decision: delete `core_level` from `backend/app/models.py`'s `UserNikkeState`, from the frontend's `UserNikkeState`/`NikkeDraft`/`CONSTRAINTS`, and from the `Core level` `NumberField` in `NikkeCard`. Note the game rule this leaves implicit: core enhancement only unlocks after 3 breakthroughs (grade), so a real "core level" is bounded by grade — the `InvestmentBadge` deliberately does NOT enforce or validate that relationship, since it is a read-only display of whatever the sync/import payload reported, not a data-entry field a user could get wrong.
- Why: an input the backend reads nowhere is worse than no input at all — it implies the number matters and invites the user to "fix" a value that has zero effect on the recommendation. This is the identical shape of problem `pve_cube` was (per-unit cube input removed once the harmony-cube work made it noise), and the same fix applies: remove the dead input, let the read-only badge carry the real number.
- Consequences: `UserNikkeState`/`NikkeDraft`/`NikkeDraftErrors`/`CONSTRAINTS.core_level` and the `Core level` field are gone; a roster stored in `localStorage` before this change still carries a stale `core_level` key per entry, which loads fine (pydantic's `extra="ignore"` default on `UserNikkeState` means an unexpected key like this was always silently dropped rather than validated, so removing it changes nothing at parse time either). `exiaImport.ts`'s `limit_break.core` mapping (the only other producer of `core_level`) was deleted rather than repointed at the badge fields — the ExiaInvasion import path was never wired to populate `grade`/`core` in the first place, so this is a pre-existing gap, not a regression, and out of scope here.

## Superior code damage now gates on elemental advantage; the fix broke zero tests, and mutation testing found only one test covered the stat at all
- Date: 2026-07-20
- Context: `damage_formula.py`'s element bonus group added `other_elemental_bonus` ("Superior Code Damage") unconditionally — `element_bonus_damage = element_multiplier + other_elemental_bonus` — but in game that stat only applies when the attacker holds elemental advantage. Discovered while wiring the harmony cube's assumed Lv.15 effects (below): the cube's anti-code skill supplies exactly this stat to every unit, so leaving the bug in place would have expanded a pre-existing, unit-scale overstatement into a roster-wide one the moment the cube shipped.
- Decision: gate the term on `element_multiplier > 1.0` (already the advantage indicator — no new parameter needed): `element_bonus_damage = element_multiplier + (other_elemental_bonus if has_element_advantage else 0.0)`.
- Why: this is what "우월 코드 공격 대미지 ▲" means in game — advantage-conditional, not universal. Several individually-encoded skills (`maiden_ice_rose`, `marciana_marine_study`, `guillotine_winter_slayer`) had already self-gated this exact condition inside their own modules, which is corroborating evidence the formula-level gate was the actual bug, not a modeling choice those units happened to disagree with.
- Consequences: measured against trunk (1004 passed) before landing, the gate broke **zero** existing tests — not because the term was safe, but because nothing exercised it: mutation-testing the fix (making `other_elemental_bonus` inert entirely) failed only 1 of 1004 tests, and that one test's boss was itself advantaged, so it couldn't have caught a missing gate either. The non-advantaged case had no test coverage at all, which is why the bug survived until the cube forced the question. ~~Units that already self-gated now double-gate (harmless, same condition twice) — their existing gates were deliberately left in place rather than cleaned up, since removing them is unrelated to this fix.~~ A regression test pinning both the neutral (no bonus) and advantaged (bonus applies) cases now exists in `backend/tests/test_raid_simulator.py`.
- **Amendment, 2026-07-20 (same day, later):** the struck sentence above was wrong, and it is the exact reasoning error that produced a real bug. "Double-gate, harmless" assumed every self-gate's condition coincides with the formula's advantage gate — that has to be verified per unit against the actual element cycle, not assumed. It held for `anis_sparkling_summer`, `maiden_ice_rose`, `marciana_marine_study`, `guillotine_winter_slayer`, `elegg_boom_and_shock`, and `privaty` (checked). It did NOT hold for `rei_ayanami`: her self-gate was `boss_is_element("Iron")`, justified as "Fire > Iron", but `elements.py`'s real cycle is Water>Fire>Wind>Iron>Electric>Water — Fire beats Wind, not Iron. Her gate was mutually exclusive with the formula's advantage gate rather than coincident with it, so after this fix landed her buff became unreachable under every boss (fixed separately, see `rei_ayanami.py` and the roadmap's gap #5 follow-up entry). Corrected rule: a per-unit boss gate is only redundant-and-harmless when it is checked, unit by unit, to name the same boss the formula's advantage gate would already select for that unit's element — never assumed from a plausible-sounding "X > Y" note.

## Harmony cube modeled as a global "everyone wears Resilience Lv.15" assumption, not per-unit input or an engine-chosen optimum
- Date: 2026-07-20
- Context: harmony cube effects had never once reached the simulation for any unit (see the insights.md entry this decision resolves) — `roster._passive_effects` read percent keys off a `PveCube` model that only ever had `name`/`level`, so the effect builder always returned an empty list. Separately, the per-unit cube INPUT itself was noise: a cube type can only be worn by 12 units at once, but decks re-equip between fights, so in solo raid all 25 roster units fight with a cube on — collection-time equip state doesn't reflect that (128 of Fienn's 159 collected units showed as uncubed, an artifact of when the snapshot was taken, not of how they actually fight).
- Alternatives considered: (a) **keep per-unit input**, just wire it correctly — rejected, since it would still encode a snapshot fact (worn/not-worn at collection time) that has no bearing on how the unit fights in solo raid; (b) **engine picks the optimal cube per unit** — the theoretically correct model, since free re-equipment makes cube choice a deck-building decision variable rather than a fixed input, exactly like burst-tier seating already is; rejected FOR NOW because it requires collecting the full cube catalogue (today only one cube, Resilience, has measured percentages) and multiplies per-unit evaluation cost in deck search, neither of which this pass needed to unblock; (c) **global one cube type, assumed Lv.15 for everyone** — CHOSEN.
- Decision (Fienn, 2026-07-20): (c). Every unit is assumed to wear a Resilience Cube (렐릭 베어 큐브) at Lv.15; the collected/synced equip state is discarded entirely, on both the effect path (`cube_effects.assumed_cube_effects`) and the sync path's flat stats (`roster_assembly`'s `harmony_cube_lv` forced to `ASSUMED_CUBE_LEVEL`). Per-unit cube selection is deferred as the next extension once demand justifies the catalogue-collection cost.
- Why: this closes the actual gap in scope for this pass — the effect NEVER reaching the simulation, for any unit, on any path — without taking on the cost of the fully-correct optimizer, and it removes noise (collection-time equip state) that the per-unit-input model couldn't have distinguished from a real modeling fact anyway. The percentages themselves are derived from the committed stat table (`tables.json`'s `resilience_cube`, renamed from `cube_sample`) rather than hardcoded — reload speed 29.69%, superior code damage 19.09% at Lv.15, confirmed against the in-game tooltip by Fienn on 2026-07-20 (see the companion insight in `docs/insights.md` for how the table's `level1`/`level2`/`level3` arrays actually encode this).
- Consequences: `models.PveCube`, `UserNikkeState.pve_cube`, `NikkeSpec.cube`, `cube_effects.cube_to_effects`, and the frontend's cube input (`PveCubeField`, `hasCube`/`pve_cube`/`cubeKnown` on `NikkeDraft`) are all removed — there is no cube state left to carry. The UI states "All Nikkes are simulated wearing a Resilience Cube Lv.15." **The manual stat-entry path stays inconsistent as a result**, and this was accepted rather than fixed here: its ATK is a single typed number the user copies off ShiftyPad, which cannot be decomposed into terms, so a unit whose ATK was read while genuinely uncubed is now understated by the Lv.15 assumption's flat contribution (~2.7% low against the collected roster's median raid400 ATK of 102,205, vs. the cube's flat +2,780 ATK / +83,400 HP at Lv.15). The sync path has no such gap — it computes ATK/HP from itemised terms, so swapping in the assumed level costs nothing extra. Removing the manual-entry path entirely (to close this gap at the root) was considered and deliberately deferred as a separate, larger product decision — see the design spec's "알려진 한계" section (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-20-harmony-cube-assumed-lv15-design.md`) for the ~800-1,200 LOC scope estimate and why a 2.7% error doesn't justify deciding it here. Implementation: `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-20-harmony-cube-assumed-lv15.md`, branch `wip/harmony-cube-lv15`; final state 1008 backend tests / 155 frontend tests passing, tsc and lint clean.
## 완성도 배치: 낡은 보류 3건 정리 + Scarlet 즉시재장 + 라운드 그랜트 스택 캡
- Date: 2026-07-20
- Context: 미인코딩이 0명이 된 뒤, ⚠ 등급 42명의 보류 사유를 전수 조사했다. 대부분은 의도적 defer(생존/힐, `hit_rate`·Explosion Radius 같은 inert 스탯, 보스 1기라 무의미한 조건, 펠릿 카운터)였지만, **엔진이 이미 지원하는데 모듈 docstring이 낡아서 남아 있던 것**이 섞여 있었다.
- Decision: 네 건을 처리했다. ① **Chisato·Neon** — 둘 다 docstring에 "pulse 경로엔 damage_type이 없다"고 적혀 있었으나 `instant_nuke_pulse_rule`의 `damage_type`은 2026-07-18 Scarlet 배치에서 배선됐고 bready·scarlet이 이미 쓰고 있었다. Chisato의 48노멀 넉을 `true`로(엔진이 true에 한해 `enemy_def=0`을 적용하고 `true_damage_up` 버킷을 태우므로 **이중 언더카운트**였다 — 그녀는 자기 배틀스타트 진댐+48.62%도 못 받고 있었다), Neon의 Firepower Explosion 2건을 `projectile_explosion`으로 교정. 후자는 그녀 단독으론 무영향이지만 PE를 버프하는 덱(Rapi: Red Hood)에서 저평가를 만들고 있었다. ② **Zwei** — 풀버스트 창 게이팅 per-shot 트리거가 없다는 보류였으나 `every_during_full_burst`는 2026-07-15에 들어왔다. 착수 전 "Frame Analysis는 'Pierce Attacks 101 상태' 게이팅이라 무기변형에 묶여 여전히 막혔을 것"이라고 추정했는데 **원문 확인 결과 틀렸다**: "Pierce Attacks 101"은 Overcharge Formula 두 번째 불릿의 **아군 버프 이름**이지 무기변형이 아니다. 둘 다 인코딩(E2E 스팟체크 +30.6%). ③ **Scarlet** — Asura의 "풀버스트 진입 시 매거진 100% 재장전"이 gap #11 계열로 보류돼 있었는데, 그 갭이 같은 날 해소됐다. **길이 0짜리 세그먼트**가 시간도 샷도 소비하지 않으면서 다음 구간을 새 탄창으로 시작시키므로 즉시 재장전이 된다(엔진 변경 0). 자체딜 +17.8%, 시퀀스 발동 +10.3%.
- ④ **라운드 그랜트 스택 캡 (엔진 변경)**: Zwei의 Pierce Equation은 "stacks up to 3"인데 `RoundGrant`에 캡 개념이 없어 미적용이었다. 증상이 수신자 케이던스 의존이라 눈에 안 띄었다 — 탄창 무기 아군은 Zwei보다 빨리 쏴서 1개만 들고 있지만, **차지 무기(RL/SR) 아군은 재장전 공백 뒤 첫 샷에서 최대 5개를 한꺼번에 받는다**. 이건 과대평가이고 이 프로젝트의 floor 관례에 어긋나므로, 인코딩을 그대로 두지 않고 `RoundGrant`에 opt-in `cap`/`cap_group`을 추가해 **수신자별로** 최신 `cap`개만 남기도록 했다(가득 찬 스택에 새로 들어오면 가장 오래된 것이 밀려나는 게임 시맨틱과 일치하고, 버프가 원래보다 길게 유지되지 않는 방향).
- Why: ①②는 "엔진이 못 한다"가 아니라 **문서가 코드보다 낡았던** 경우다. 프리미티브가 추가될 때 그것을 기다리던 기존 보류를 되짚지 않으면, 이미 가능한 일이 계속 defer로 남는다. ④는 반대 방향의 교훈 — 새 인코딩이 낙관 편향을 들여왔을 때 "그래도 없는 것보단 낫다"로 넘기지 않는다.
- Consequences: ✅ 34 → 35명(Zwei). 캡은 opt-in이라 기존 무캡 소비자 5명(ada-wong·ein·jill-valentine·miranda·zwei의 다른 그랜트)은 타임라인이 바이트 단위로 동일함을 데미지 로그 해시 대조로 확인했다. 남은 구조적 보류는 Normal-Attack-only 버킷 · 펠릿 카운터 · 파츠 파괴 이벤트 · Pierce 키워드 — 전부 별도 설계가 필요하다.

## gap #11(강제 재장전/탄약 제거)은 신규 발사-타임라인 프리미티브 없이 해소 — 샷 0개 세그먼트 + 재장전 공식 양방향화 + `burst_anchored_buffs`
- Date: 2026-07-20
- Context: Milk: Blooming Bunny가 마지막 미인코딩 유닛이었고, 2026-07-17 이후 "발사 타임라인 자체를 스킬이 조작(규모 중~대)"으로 적혀 있던 gap #11에 막혀 있었다. 그녀의 Embarrassment는 진입 시 **탄약 100% 제거 + 강제 재장전(속도 50% 감소)**을 건다.
- Alternatives considered: (a) 기록대로 발사 타임라인에 개입하는 재장전 이벤트 프리미티브를 신설. (b) Embarrassment를 통째로 defer하고 버프만 얇게 인코딩 — 그 상태가 킷의 심장이라 정직하지 않고, 재장전 다운타임을 빼면 **낙관 방향**으로 틀린다(ceiling). (c) 상태를 정적 무기 프로필(탄창 1발)로 접기 — 착수 초기에 내가 제안했다가 **Fienn 정정으로 폐기**: 그녀는 상시 루프가 아니라 자기 버스트 사이클과 교대한다. (d) 기존 프리미티브 조합.
- Decision: (d). 세 가지 사실이 확장 규모를 무너뜨렸다. ① 진입 조건이 "상태가 아닐 때"이고 Fienn 판정(2026-07-20)상 **버스트 면역으로만 해제**되므로, 진입은 자기 버스트 1회당 정확히 1회 — 매 풀차지 루프가 아니다. ② `_base_shot_records`가 각 구간을 `window_start`에서 **새 탄창으로 재시작**하므로(무기변형 v1의 resume 시맨틱), "탄약 제거 + 강제 재장전"은 곧 **샷 0개짜리 `weapon_mode_schedules` 세그먼트**다. ③ 재장전 속도 조작은 `reload_speed_percent` 콜러블로 이미 배선돼 있었다.
- 실제로 필요했던 소규모 확장 2건: **`reload_time_with_speed`** — 속도 감소를 시간 증가의 거울로 모델(`speed ≥ 0 → base/(1+s)`, `speed < 0 → base×(1−s)`). 기존 공식은 −50%에서 4초를 줬지만 Fienn 인게임 수치는 그녀의 2초 기본에 대해 **3초**다. 기존 소비자가 전부 +버프뿐이라 이 오류가 드러난 적이 없었다(음수 분기는 관측 한 점에만 앵커돼 있음을 docstring에 명시). **`burst_anchored_buffs`** — 버스트 시각 + 오프셋에 버프를 걸고 지속시간을 `UNTIL_NEXT_OWN_BURST`로 지정할 수 있는 소형 패스. `own_burst_activate` 규칙으로는 불가능한데, 그 규칙이 발동하는 시점엔 버스트 사이클 순회가 진행 중이라 **다음 버스트 시각이 아직 없기 때문**이다. 버스트 시각이 확정된 직후이자 샷 루프 **이전**에 배치해, 여기 놓인 버프가 데미지뿐 아니라 탄창/재장전/케이던스 콜러블에도 보이게 했다.
- Why: 세그먼트 프로필에 `charge_time`이 아니라 `rate_of_fire`를 쓴 것도 의도적이다 — 명시 rate 프로필은 케이던스 버프를 받지 않으므로, 아군 차지속도 버프가 빈 창을 줄여 유령 샷을 흘리는 경로가 구조적으로 막힌다.
- Consequences: **수집된 유닛 전원 인코딩 완료(미인코딩 0명).** E2E 검증: 강제재장 창에 샷 0개, 직전에 끊기고 창 종료 후 차지 1회를 거쳐 새 탄창으로 재개. Elegg·Mihara의 Pattern B 오분류와 같은 교훈이 반복됐다 — "엔진이 못 한다"를 실제 운용 빈도와 기존 프리미티브의 부수 효과에 대조하기 전에 확정하면 확장 규모를 크게 과대평가한다. 보류로 남은 건 Pierce 키워드(엔진에 per-hit pierce 플래그 없음, 기존 관례)뿐.

## "N회 크리티컬 히트 후" 트리거를 기대-크리 누적기(`every_n_critical_hits`)로 해소 — 2026-07-12의 "영구 defer" 판정 철회
- Date: 2026-07-20
- Context: EVE의 주력 딜 Unstable Energy가 "노멀공격으로 크리티컬 44회 적중" 트리거다. 2026-07-12 Julia 시그니처(Crescendo/Marcato) 인코딩 때 이 문구를 **영구 defer**로 판정했었다 — 엔진이 크리를 기대값으로만 다루고(히트마다 `crit_rate` 배율) per-hit RNG를 굴리지 않으므로 "이 샷이 크리였나"라는 이벤트 자체가 없기 때문. 그 판정이 맞다면 EVE는 킷의 심장이 빠진 얇은 인코딩만 가능했다.
- Alternatives considered: (a) 판정 유지, 영구 defer — Unstable Energy(240%×3연타)와 Electric 받댐 라이더를 통째로 누락. (b) 빌드시점 고정 발수 환산 — 44 / 0.75 = 59발마다. 단순하고 확장 불필요하지만 **덱의 크리율 버프를 전부 무시**한다(Miranda·Zwei·Julia 등이 있어도 발동 빈도가 그대로). (c) 샷마다 라이브 크리율을 누적하는 기대-크리 누적기. (d) 실제 per-hit 크리 RNG 도입 — 엔진의 결정론적 기대값 모델을 통째로 갈아엎는 일이라 검토만.
- Decision (Fienn, 2026-07-20): (c). Fienn의 수용 조건이 정확히 "기대값으로 계산하는 건 합리적인데, 덱에 크리율 버프가 적용이 안 되는 건 문제"였고, 이게 (b)를 배제하고 (c)를 규정한다. `per_shot_rules`에 `every_n_critical_hits` 모드 추가(`raid_simulator.py`): 샷마다 `min(1.0, base_crit_rate + registry.total_for("crit_rate", target, shot_time))`을 누적기에 더하고, 임계 N을 넘을 때마다 발동시키며 나머지를 이월한다.
- Why: 이벤트를 새로 만드는 게 아니라 **딜 경로가 이미 하고 있는 기대값 환산을 카운터에도 똑같이 적용**하는 것이라 엔진의 크리 철학과 일관된다. 라이브 조회라 덱 크리 버프가 자연스럽게 발동 케이던스를 바꾼다 — 엔진 테스트로 고정(50%→100% 크리율이 발동 간격을 정확히 절반으로 만든다).
- Consequences: EVE 인코딩 가능. **Julia(시그니처)의 Crescendo/Marcato도 같은 날 인코딩 완료 — "영구 defer" 딱지가 떨어졌다. 단 per-shot 모드만으로는 부족했다: Crescendo가 캡 5 스택이라 같은 누적기를 쓰는 자원 fill `("per_critical_hit_every", N)`를 함께 추가했고, 두 경로는 `_expected_crit_positions`를 공유한다**(`docs/insights.md`의 해당 항목도 정정됨 — 그 문서에 남아 있던 "not a to-do for a future extension"은 이제 틀린 서술이었다). 알려진 한계 2건: ① 샷 루프가 유닛별로 돌기 때문에 **나중에 처리되는 아군의 per-shot 규칙이 거는 크리 버프는 안 보인다**(버스트/풀버스트 트리거 크리 버프는 모든 샷 루프보다 먼저 등록되므로 보임 — 통상적인 크리 버퍼는 대부분 여기 해당). ② 누적 비교에 `1e-9` 허용오차가 필요하다 — 0.3을 10번 더하면 2.9999999999999996이 되어 발동이 한 샷 밀린다(`burst_cycle`의 `last + cooldown` 반올림 함정과 같은 계열).

## Phase 7 sub-project 4: the caller-side session comes from the USER's own browser via a bookmarklet, not an operator account; roster storage stays client-side; sub-project 3 (ownership proof) is dissolved
- Date: 2026-07-19
- Context: the Stage 1 reconnaissance entry below established that a caller-side authenticated blablalink session can read any consenting public account's roster, but explicitly deferred "who supplies the caller-side session" for a hosted service. Fienn confirmed the near-term goal IS a public hosted service for other users, so this had to be answered against the project's standing "never store credentials, tokens supplied by Fienn" posture.
- Alternatives considered: (a) **operator session, server-side** — the service holds its own blablalink account and calls the API on every user's behalf. Best UX (paste a share URL, done; works on mobile) but rejected: it means storing operator cookies server-side (violates the credential posture, for the operator rather than the target); whether a game-unlinked burner account even works is UNVERIFIED (anonymous calls are refused with code 300001 "game not login", suggesting a real game-linked account may be required); session expiry stops ALL users at once until Fienn manually re-extracts cookies; one account querying hundreds of distinct open_ids is exactly the traffic shape that reads as scraping; and a ban on that single account kills the entire service — every one of these unknowns is service-fatal and outside our control. (b) **user's own session via a bookmarklet** — CHOSEN. (c) **a browser extension** (the ExiaInvasion precedent) — more capable for repeat sync, but carries store-review and maintenance burden not needed once the bookmarklet was proven to work.
- Decision (Fienn, 2026-07-19): (b). The user pastes their ShiftyPad share URL once; we generate a personalised bookmarklet containing only their own `intl_open_id` (no credential); they click it on a blablalink page, where it calls the three collector endpoints with their OWN session cookies and hands the raw payloads to our app via `window.open` + `postMessage`; our stateless backend assembles them and the result merges into localStorage. Storage model follows from this: the roster lives in the browser only, the assemble endpoint persists nothing, and there is no account system or login. An anonymous client id (localStorage UUID, analytics only, never sent to blablalink, never stored with a roster) was added for product analytics. **Sub-project 3 (ownership proof) is dissolved by this decision** — submitting your own data with your own session leaves nobody to prove ownership to; the previously-planned `profile_team` challenge is unnecessary.
- Why: postMessage rather than a direct POST from the blablalink page to our domain — a direct POST would depend on blablalink's CSP `connect-src` allowing us, which was never verified; postMessage isn't governed by `connect-src`, so this removes the unknown entirely rather than testing it. Empirically verified in a real browser (2026-07-19, not assumed): a bookmarklet runs on blablalink pages without being blocked by CSP; a credentialed fetch from that page context passes CORS and returns code 0 with complete data (the returned `recycle_room_researches` matched the test suite's ground truth exactly); `game_openid` is HttpOnly, so the bookmarklet CANNOT discover the open id itself, which is why it must be baked into the bookmarklet at generation time. Observed error codes: 300001 not logged in, 1303005 malformed/placeholder open id, 1 wrongly-formatted open id.
- Consequences: desktop only — bookmarklet installation is not realistically possible on iOS/Android, so mobile is an explicit non-goal. Changing the bookmarklet's logic forces EVERY user to re-install their bookmark, because loading an external script would be blocked by blablalink's script-src, so the logic is inline — this is why cheap correctness fixes were front-loaded before any user installed it. Storing no credentials and no rosters is a meaningful posture for a public service, at the cost of no cross-device sync and no recovery (re-syncing is two clicks, which is what makes that acceptable). Two implementation facts that corrected the design: React 19 HARD-BLOCKS `javascript:` URLs passed via a JSX `href` prop (verified in react-dom's PRODUCTION bundle, which substitutes a throwing stub), so the bookmarklet link's `href` must be written imperatively via a ref callback during commit; and `window.open` must be called BEFORE the three awaited fetches, or the click's transient user activation can expire and the popup gets blocked. Verified end to end on Fienn's real account: no popup block, 159 units imported, and a second run exercised the merge path (0 added / 159 updated), confirming previously-entered cube data survives a re-sync. Still outstanding, not blockers: a privacy policy document before any public deployment; a routine for refreshing the committed directory snapshot (a Nikke released after it is silently skipped); and deployment infrastructure (no remote exists today). Spec and plan: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-19-roster-sync-bookmarklet-design.md`, `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-19-roster-sync-bookmarklet.md`.

## Multi-user preconditions: unknown overload effect types are skipped (with a warning), unobserved overload levels are linearly filled, and missing recycle-room/outpost lookups default to rank 0
- Date: 2026-07-19
- Context: Phase 7 roster sync must assemble OTHER users' rosters (see the Stage 1 reconnaissance entry below — a caller-side session can already read a consenting public account's data), but every reference table in `backend/app/stat_assembly.py` was fitted from Fienn's single roster. Four lookups raised `KeyError` on anything the fitting pass never observed: an overload effect type outside the named set, an overload roll level outside the observed levels for its type, a corporation-research rank row, and an outpost defensive-slot lookup.
- Alternatives considered: (a) fail the sync outright on any unknown input (maximum accuracy per-value, but reproduces the exact "can't sync another user" failure this work exists to fix — any account with even one effect type or level Fienn's roster never rolled can't sync at all); (b) surface an unknown overload line to the user as "unknown effect (type N)" rather than dropping it (visible, but still unusable — the simulator maps overload lines by name, so an unnamed line can't be wired to a stat regardless); (c) skip unknown/unobserved inputs with a warning and a sane default, and assemble the rest of the roster.
- Decision (Fienn, 2026-07-19): (c). Concretely: an overload roll of an unnamed effect type is dropped from that unit's assembled stats, with a warning naming the `(type, level)` so the type/level can be added once observed; an overload roll at a level not directly in the fitted table is filled via the type's own linear fit (see the linearity insight in `docs/insights.md`) rather than raising; a missing recycle-room research row defaults to rank 0; a missing outpost defensive lookup likewise defaults to rank 0.
- Why: the level-fill case loses no accuracy at all — the overload table is provably linear per type (cross-checked, not just curve-fit smooth; see `docs/insights.md`), so any unobserved level within a type's already-named range is mathematically determined, not guessed. The remaining case (a genuinely unnamed effect type) is the only real approximation: those types are most likely DEF/hit-rate-class lines the simulator's damage path doesn't consume anyway (the ones that matter for damage — ATK, elemental damage, crit, etc. — were all named early because they're the rolls players chase), so dropping one unnamed line and warning is cheaper than failing an entire roster's sync over a probably-inert stat. The warning is the safety valve: it names the exact `(type, level)` so the table can be completed from a real observation instead of the assumption being silently load-bearing forever.
- Consequences: another user's roster now assembles instead of crashing on the first unknown value. Residual risk: if an unnamed overload type turns out to be damage-relevant after all, a synced roster silently understates that unit until the warning is investigated and the type is named — this is a known, accepted gap, not a hidden one. Implemented on branch `wip/multiuser-preconditions` (commit `dcf3fc8`); backend suite 812 passed; the existing 77/77 overload-parity check is unchanged (all of Fienn's own observed types/levels still resolve identically, since the fallbacks only engage on genuinely unseen inputs).

## Elegg: Boom and Shock의 버스트를 고스트 캡까지 보류 — `burst_delay`가 `min_interval`을 갖게 됨
- Date: 2026-07-19
- Context: 13 Ghosts는 13스택(캡)에서 13히트, 미만이면 6히트만 나간다. 쿨마다(40초) 발동시키자 시뮬은 전투당 5번 버스트했고 그중 4번이 6히트 분기, 첫 발은 t=2에 고스트 3개뿐이었다. Fienn의 실제 플레이 서술은 "전투 중 2번, 그것도 13스택이 쌓인 후".
- Decision: `not_before = cap × capture_interval`(= 78초, 최초 캡 도달 시각), `min_interval = spend_at_cap × capture_interval`(= 54초, 버스트로 소모된 뒤 다시 캡까지 채워지는 시간)을 그녀 자신의 스킬 값에서 유도해 `burst_delay`에 싣는다(`app/skill_rules/elegg_boom_and_shock.py::build_elegg_burst_delay`, `registry.py`의 `_BURST_DELAY_BUILDERS`). 하드코딩한 측정치가 아니라 빌더 함수 — Diesel의 지연은 고정값(`_DIESEL_BURST_DELAY`)이라 다른 이유로 `get_burst_delay`가 빌더 형태를 취함(값이 유닛 스킬값에서 유도되는 경우까지 커버).
- Why: 54초 재충전이 40초 쿨보다 길어서 **실효 케이던스는 쿨다운이 아니라 재충전**이다. 결과: 78.2초 / 136.2초 2번 버스트, 둘 다 13스택 — Fienn의 서술과 일치.
- 구현 세부(`burst_cycle.py::_ready_at`): `min_interval`은 CDR로 되감기는 `last_used_at`이 아니라 새로 추가한 `last_fired_at`(CDR에 영향받지 않는 실제 발동 시각 사본)에서 잰다 — 고스트는 벽시계로 차고, 아군 쿨감이 그걸 앞당기지 못하기 때문. 이걸 `last_used_at` 기준으로 뒀을 때 54초 간격이 39초로 3번 줄어드는 회귀가 있었다(고쳐서 병합).
- Consequence: "종료 직전 1번" 같은 부가 운용은 모델링하지 않음(Fienn이 먼저 말한 "2번"이 일반형이라 YAGNI). 지연된 유닛이 자기 티어에 혼자면 그 사이클은 아예 안 뜬다(조기 발동으로 credit하는 것보다 정직 — 아래 `burst_delay` 엔진 결정과 공통).

## `burst_cycle`에 유닛별 버스트 지연(`burst_delay`) 도입 — 스킵/보류/최소간격 3형태
- Date: 2026-07-19
- Context: 2026-07-17에 "Diesel은 짝수 사이클 버스트가 강한데 `burst_cycle`이 '한 사이클 거르기'를 표현 못 한다"로 미뤄둔 갭. Diesel 인코딩 시점에 착수하기로 했던 항목이고, 이번에 Diesel과 Elegg 두 소비자가 생겨 구현.
- Decision: `burst_cycle`의 덱 멤버가 선택적 `burst_delay`를 가진다. 세 형태: `{"skip_cycles": N}`(앞 N사이클에서 그 유닛을 제외), `{"not_before": T}`(T초 전 발동 금지), `{"min_interval": S}`(실효 쿨다운을 S로 늘림). 세 형태 모두 멤버별 ready time을 계산하는 한 곳(`burst_cycle.py::_ready_at`)에 접혀 들어가, 티어 선택(`tier_ready_time`)과 eligibility 체크가 지금까지와 동일한 산술을 그대로 쓴다 — 기존 fractional-CDR 반올림 회귀 수정이 그대로 유지됨.
- Why 두 축을 다 두는가: Diesel의 상태는 사이클 카운트가 근거이고(그녀가 첫 풀버스트에 참여했는지), Elegg는 자원(고스트) 충전 시간이 근거다 — 서로 환산 불가능한데, 덱 구성에 따라 사이클 길이 자체가 달라지기 때문이다.
- Consequence: 지연된 유닛이 자기 티어에 혼자면 사이클이 아예 안 뜬다(조기 발동으로 없는 상태를 credit하는 것보다 정직) — `ALLOWED_SHAPES`가 (1,1,3)/(1,2,2)/(2,1,2)뿐이라 Burst 3 티어는 항상 2명 이상이라 실제로는 발생하지 않는다(Fienn 지적). `min_interval` 세부(측정 기준점이 `last_fired_at`이어야 하는 이유)는 아래 Elegg 항목, `skip_cycles` 세부는 아래 Diesel 항목 참고.

## Diesel: Winter Sweets를 Intro/Highlight 2슬러그(`MODE_VARIANTS`)로 인코딩, Highlight는 실제로 첫 사이클을 건너뜀
- Date: 2026-07-19
- Context: 그녀는 첫 풀버스트에서 자기 버스트를 쐈는지(Intro) 안 쐈는지(Highlight)로 상태가 확정되고 **전투 종료까지 고정**된다(Fienn 확인 2026-07-19, 전투 중 변경 불가). Highlight의 지속딜 버프가 235.03%인 반면 Intro는 60.19% — 약 4배 차이.
- Decision: `registry.MODE_VARIANTS`에 `diesel-winter-sweets-intro` / `diesel-winter-sweets-highlight` 2슬러그로 등록. Highlight 슬러그에는 `_BURST_DELAY_BUILDERS`를 통해 `{"skip_cycles": 1}` burst_delay를 실제로 건다.
- Why 이게 Bready와 다른가(핵심 판단): Bready의 Taste 분기는 엔진이 시뮬레이션하지 않는 축(받는 버프의 종류)으로 갈리므로 정적 `MODE_VARIANTS` 슬러그만으로 충분하다. **Diesel의 상태는 엔진이 실제로 시뮬레이션하는 축 — 버스트 스케줄 — 으로 갈린다.** 그래서 Highlight 슬러그를 정적 버프만으로 만들면 "1사이클에 버스트하면서 Highlight 버프까지 챙기는" 상태를 만들어 과대평가하게 된다. 실제로 `skip_cycles: 1`을 걸어 그 사이클엔 그녀가 버스트하지 않도록 강제해야 정직한 결과가 나온다.
- 결과 검증: Intro는 홀수 사이클(2.2, 27.0, 52.3…), Highlight는 짝수 사이클(14.4, 39.6, 64.9…)에 버스트하고 덱의 풀버스트 횟수는 15로 동일. 180초 총딜 기준 Highlight가 약 7.8% 우위 — Fienn의 "짝수 사이클이 확실히 강했다"는 플레이 경험과 일치.
- 모델된 것: 상태별 영구 Critical Damage와 풀버스트당 Sustained Damage 버프; Full Charge 스택(`per_shot_every 1`, `ResourceSpec(cap=2, lifetime=3.0)` — RL은 매 발사가 풀차지이므로 별도 카운터 불필요); 풀버스트 DoT(`full_burst_windows` 앵커); 버스트의 Damage Taken 디버프와 그 9틱 DoT(전체 적 18.43% + 스테이지 타겟 181.2% 합산, 레이드=보스 1기 취급); 부위파괴 Sustained 버프는 `boss_part_destructible` 플로어/실링 브래킷으로.
- 보류(중요 — 낙관 편향): Noise Pollution(Highlight 상태에서 아군 Hit Rate −100%/1초)과 그걸 막는 Mute 스택 둘 다 미모델 — 엔진이 `hit_rate`를 소비하지 않아 인코딩해도 inert. 실제 게임에선 Highlight의 이득이 아군 명중 페널티를 대가로 치르는데, 여기선 대가 없이 이득만 반영된다. Highlight 덱은 "Mute 관리를 제대로 하는 플레이 전제"로 읽을 것.

## Deck search now scores every intra-tier ordering per combination, not a canonical order refined for a top-K shortlist — closes a mis-scoring gap measured up to 78%
- Date: 2026-07-19
- Context: `search_best_decks` ranked each shape combination on ONE arbitrary ("canonical") intra-tier order, then only permuted the top 40 combinations by that canonical score (`permutation_top_k`) to find the true best order. This treated order as a tie-break — but `simulate_burst_cycle` fires the FIRST ready member of each burst tier, so order decides which tier member never bursts at all. That is a real strategy, not an artifact: a (1,1,3)'s rightmost Burst 3 commonly runs as a buffer/normal-attack unit that never bursts, Prika must burst before Mint for her Encore to hand Mint the slot, and Velvet / Helm: Aquamarine are usually seated without ever bursting (Fienn, 2026-07-19). The new measurement script `scripts/measure_intra_tier_ordering_impact.py` quantified the damage: of 56 measured (1,1,3) combinations, 9 swing over 10% between canonical and best order (worst case 78%), and the TRUE best deck for that dataset ranked #21 under canonical scoring — outside the top-40 shortlist that ever got permuted, so the old search could not have surfaced it under any budget.
- Decision: `search_best_decks` (`backend/app/deck_search.py`, commit `ed9ef0a`) now scores EVERY intra-tier ordering of EVERY shape combination — `_all_intra_tier_orderings` replaces the canonical-score-then-refine-top-K flow. `permutation_top_k` is removed entirely; `sim_budget` now counts orderings (what actually costs sims) rather than combinations, so the existing pool-pruning fallback (`prune_candidate_pool`) still triggers correctly when the full ordering count would blow the budget.
- Why: intra-tier order is not a tie-break between equivalent decks, it's a role assignment that changes which unit contributes at all — so ranking a combination on a single order can mis-score it by exactly the amount that order differs from the best one, and a fixed top-K shortlist compounds the error by only ever fixing the mistake for the K combinations that happened to look best under the wrong order in the first place. Scoring every ordering is the only way to rank combinations on their true achievable value; the measured 78%-worst-case / rank-#21 result shows this was not a marginal gap.
- Consequences: `permutation_top_k` is gone — any caller that passed it now gets a `TypeError` rather than a silent behavior change (none did outside tests, which were updated in the same commit). Cost is now driven by ordering count, not combination count — a (1,1,3) costs 3! orderings, a (1,2,2) costs 2!×2!, both scored in full whenever they fit inside `sim_budget`, else the roster is pruned first via `prune_candidate_pool`. `scripts/measure_intra_tier_ordering_impact.py` is kept as a reusable tool, not a one-off — rerun it after changing `burst_cycle`, the deck search's scoring, or encoding a unit whose value depends on NOT bursting, to confirm ordering sensitivity is still handled and to re-derive the numbers above. See the companion insight in `docs/insights.md` ("Deck search").

## Snow White: Heavy Arms needed no new engine primitive — closes the weapon-transform spec's deferred verification pass
- Date: 2026-07-19
- Context: the weapon-transform design spec (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-18-weapon-transform-design.md`) had deferred a verification pass on her charge-loop kit as possibly needing a new state machine (lock-on/ammo accrual, an Auto-Fire-on-every-full-charge pattern layered with a burst-triggered empowered window).
- Decision: land her (`backend/app/skill_rules/snow_white_heavy_arms.py`, commit `544e8d3`) entirely on existing primitives. Auto Fire (fires on every full charge, in or out of her burst window) rides `per_shot_rules`' `every`/`every_during_segment`/`every_outside_segment` modes; Seven Dwarves Fully Active (her burst) is an ordinary `weapon_mode_schedules` segment — 2 shots at a fixed 3.2s charge (`until_shots=2`) — with Shades of White's +528% Charge Damage folded directly into the segment profile's `charge_damage_percent` (added on top of her own base 250%, via a new `caster_weapon_stats` injection into skill-value assembly) so deck-wide Charge Damage/ATK buffs still multiply the segment in phase 2 instead of being baked into a constant. Lock-on/ammo accrual (0.2s ticks toward a 5-round base / 15-round boosted cap) is a per-mode CONSTANT, not a live counter, because the ticks always reach the cap well within the charge time (6 ticks in the 1.2s base charge, 16 in the 3.2s boosted charge).
- Why (Fienn's rulings, 2026-07-19): Auto Fire's sequential hits all land on the single raid boss, matching this engine's existing single-boss convention for Lock-On kits; the lock-on "Damage Taken +4.2%/4s" tick is approximated as a permanent squad-scope debuff rather than a real tick-and-decay cycle, since charging uptime is effectively continuous and the 4s buff duration far exceeds the 0.2s re-application interval.
- Consequences: closes the last item on plan 2's backlog — no unit in the weapon-transform scope ended up needing a primitive beyond `weapon_mode_schedules` (plan 1) and the `every_during_segment`/`every_outside_segment` per-shot modes (plan 2; see the companion insight in `docs/insights.md` on the record-identity trap those modes had to be fixed for). `docs/engine-gaps.md` and the skill's `references/special-mechanics.md` reflect the now-closed backlog.

## Rapi: Red Hood's Combat Assist branch is now a real Burst-1 candidate (`rapi-red-hood-b1`), excluded from any deck holding another Burst-1 unit
- Date: 2026-07-19
- Context: her Combat Assist branch (Battlefield Assessment's `no_other_burst_tier_allies(1)` gate) was already encoded and evaluated per-deck, but every candidate deck seated her at her nominal Burst tier 3 — `deck_search.py`'s own module docstring names this exact case as a known simplification ("flexible-burst units ... are placed by their nominal burst_tier for feasibility"). So Combat Assist could only ever fire in a deck that happened to lack a real Burst-1 unit; the search itself could never choose to use her as the B1.
- Decision: register `rapi-red-hood-b1` as a `MODE_VARIANTS` sibling of `rapi-red-hood` (see the mode-variant decision below) with `VARIANT_BURST_TIERS["rapi-red-hood-b1"] = 1`. Her Stage 1 (not Stage 3) Power of Inheritance burst becomes support-only: self burst-cooldown -20s (so she re-bursts every cycle from cycle 2 on, keeping pace with a real B1's cadence) and a squad flat-ATK buff worth 18.01% of her own ATK for 10s (Crown's caster-ATK precedent) — no damage. `deck_search.SOLE_TIER1_SLUGS = {"rapi-red-hood-b1"}` / `_tier1_seating_valid` excludes her from any deck holding another Burst-1 unit, checked at both enumeration paths (`shape_combinations`, `feasible_orderings`).
- Why: in real play, Combat Assist cancels itself the moment a real Burst-1 ally is present — seating `rapi-red-hood-b1` alongside another B1 would simulate a formation the game never produces, which the sole-tier1 constraint structurally prevents rather than relying on the branch's own in-game condition to zero it out at simulation time. This partially resolves `deck_search.py`'s "flexible-burst units placed by nominal tier" simplification for her specific case — `VARIANT_BURST_TIERS` now gives her a genuine tier-1 nominal slot — though the general problem (a unit choosing its burst tier dynamically per deck, without a MODE_VARIANTS-style split) remains open for any future case (e.g. Anis: Star's re-entry, also named in that same docstring).
- Consequences: getting a cross-tier variant (a B1↔B3 sibling pair) safely through `prune_candidate_pool`'s internal reference-deck construction took four follow-up fixes over commits `2eb7490`..`487aa25` (keep siblings out of swap-in measurement, restrict swap-in to the unit's own tier, measure cross-tier candidates instead of starving them at score 0.0, reject clashing alternatives when building a cross-tier reference deck) — see the companion insight in `docs/insights.md` ("Deck search"). `docs/encoded-nikkes.md`'s Rapi row records the Stage 1 branch and the constraint.

## Mode-variant dual-slug expansion: one owned character can fan out to multiple deck candidates — a play/formation-choice mechanism distinct from `-signature` dual-slot units
- Date: 2026-07-19
- Context: plan 2 of the weapon-transform work (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-18-weapon-transform-design.md`) needed to represent two kinds of "one owned character, more than one way to seat her" cases as real, independently-competing deck candidates: Cinderella: Crystal Wave's pre-battle MG/Snipe mode choice, and Rapi: Red Hood's Combat Assist Burst-1 stand-in (see the companion decision above).
- Decision: `registry.MODE_VARIANTS` (`backend/app/skill_rules/registry.py`, commit `fc0287d`) maps a base slug to the tuple of candidate slugs the roster loader (`user_roster.load_roster`) fans one owned `UserNikkeState` out to; `VARIANT_BURST_TIERS` overrides a variant's burst tier when it seats somewhere other than the character's nominal slot; `_WEAPON_PROFILE_OVERRIDE_BUILDERS`/`get_weapon_profile_override` let a variant swap in a different weapon profile once skill values resolve (Cinderella's Snipe mode is a static SR profile, not her dotgg MG stats); and `deck_search._no_variant_clash` (built from a derived slug→base map) rejects any candidate deck seating two variants of the same base, enforced at both enumeration paths (`shape_combinations`, `feasible_orderings`).
- Why this is a separate mechanism from the existing `-signature` dual-slot pattern (Drake, Julia, Laplace): `-signature` expresses the user's ITEM INVESTMENT — whether the Favorite Item is owned is a per-user fact resolved once, at roster-import time, in the frontend (`resourceIdSlugMap.ts`'s `SIGNATURE_OWNED`), and only the applicable slug is ever a candidate. A mode variant is not an ownership fact at all — it's a play/formation CHOICE the deck search itself must explore, so BOTH variants are always candidates and the search picks whichever wins; that's why it resolves in the backend roster loader instead of the frontend map.
- Consequences: the winning slug itself communicates which mode/role a recommendation used (`cinderella-crystal-wave-mg` vs `-snipe`, `rapi-red-hood` vs `-b1`) rather than needing a separate annotation. `prune_candidate_pool`'s internal reference-deck construction needed several follow-up fixes to respect the same legality invariants as real output decks once variants existed — see the companion insight in `docs/insights.md` ("Deck search").

## The outside-Full-Burst per-shot filter is closed on the right (`t <= end`), not half-open like the during-FB filter — a shot exactly at a window's end counts as inside FB
- Date: 2026-07-19
- Context: Laplace: Signature's Hero Bomber trigger uses `every_outside_full_burst` to fire only on Full Charge hits landing outside her own Full Burst window. Her weapon-mode segment's 93rd Buster tick can land exactly at `burst_time + 10.0`, which is precisely the Full Burst window's end — and `_resource_fill_times`'s outside-FB filter (`raid_simulator.py`) previously excluded the window with a half-open `start <= t < end` check (matching the existing during-FB filter), so a shot landing exactly on `end` read as "outside," spuriously firing Hero Bomber on a shot that is still, in every meaningful sense, part of the Full Burst.
- Decision: change the outside-FB filter to a closed interval, `start <= t <= end` — a shot at the window's exact end boundary counts as inside FB (matching "the last instant of Full Burst is still Full Burst," not "Full Burst just ended").
- Why: the alternative reading (end-of-window = already outside) is the one that produced the bug; the fix makes a coincidental float landing on the boundary resolve to the more defensible in-game interpretation, and is pinned by a regression test with a synthetic segment tuned to land exactly on the boundary (`test_raid_simulator.py`).
- Consequences: the during-FB filter (`per_shot_every_during_full_burst`) was deliberately left half-open (`start <= t < end`) rather than also being widened to match — widening it would double-count a boundary shot in BOTH the during and outside tallies for two different callers. The result is a narrow, intentional asymmetry: a shot landing exactly at a Full Burst window's end is caught by neither filter (not "during," per the during filter's still-open check; not "outside," per the outside filter's now-closed check). No current consumer is affected by this gap (Laplace's boundary tick is now correctly excluded from the outside filter, and nothing currently depends on it being counted as "during" either) — recorded as a known conservative asymmetry for the next per-shot-filter consumer to be aware of. See the companion insight in `docs/insights.md` ("Effects / stats").

## Weapon-mode segments v1: unify every unit's weapon pass through `generate_segmented_shots`, contract explicit `rate_of_fire` profiles as buff-free measurement anchors, and readmit `laplace-signature` as a dual-slot unit
- Date: 2026-07-19
- Context: The weapon-transform design (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-18-weapon-transform-design.md`) needed a primitive for skills that swap a unit's entire weapon profile for a window (burst-triggered cannons: Snow White, Maxwell; a sustained buffed-cadence window: Laplace's Hero Vision; and a migration target for Red Hood's existing `scheduled_nukes` approximation, which could never let deck Charge Damage/ATK buffs touch the transform because it baked the charge multiplier into a constant and subtracted an estimate of double-counted base-weapon shots inside the window). Landed as `attack_rate.generate_segmented_shots()` + `simulate_raid(..., weapon_mode_schedules=)`, commits `1643aa8`..`9b15489`. Three sub-decisions had real alternatives:
  1. **Every unit's weapon pass now runs through `generate_segmented_shots`, not just the units that actually have segments.** Alternative: an opt-in dual path (segmented generator only for the 4 consumers, legacy `generate_shot_times`/`first_bullet_shot_times`/`last_bullet_shot_times` unchanged for everyone else). Rejected — a dual path means two weapon-shot-generation code paths (magazine/reload/charge-speed reads, first/last-bullet marking) to keep in sync forever, doubling the maintenance surface for a mechanism already complex. An empty segment list is required to reproduce the legacy generator's output bit-for-bit anyway (verified by an `SR_ODD` 1.19s-charge equivalence test — see the companion insight in `docs/insights.md`), so unifying costs nothing extra once that parity is proven, and first/last-bullet markers now come straight off each `ShotRecord`'s flags instead of a separate marker recomputation (three marker-function call sites removed from `raid_simulator.py`).
  2. **An explicit `rate_of_fire` segment profile takes NO cadence buffs (deck attack-speed/charge-speed buffers do not multiply its shot count); a `charge_time` profile does.** `rate_of_fire` profiles exist specifically for windows anchored to Fienn's real-game shot-count measurement (Laplace's 93-tick Hero Vision, Red Hood's 33-shot Step 3) — the measured count already reflects whatever cadence modifiers were live during that measurement, so re-applying deck cadence buffs on top would double-count them. A `charge_time` profile (Snow White/Maxwell's cannon) is a real charge weapon, so it reads live `charge_speed_percent_at` etc. like any other weapon, and deck buffs correctly multiply it.
  3. **`laplace-signature` ships as a new dual-slot slug** (base `laplace` left unchanged), joining Drake and Julia as the only dual-slot units. This reverses an earlier project verdict that a signature weapon is a weapon *variant* and therefore can't be dual-slotted (reached during Red Hood's Pattern B investigation) — the segment primitive makes the transform genuinely representable as a bounded weapon-mode window rather than a permanent weapon change, so the reasoning behind that earlier verdict no longer applies. `SIGNATURE_OWNED` gained `100 // Laplace — Favorite Item owned` (`frontend/src/lib/resourceIdSlugMap.ts`), following the same per-user-investment pattern as Drake's entry.
- Why: (1) avoids permanent dual-maintenance for what only needed to be a one-time migration; (2) an explicit-cadence profile is fundamentally a *measured constant*, not a live-computed weapon stat — treating it like one would silently double-apply buffs that are already baked into the number; (3) the prior "signature = weapon variant, no dual-slot" reasoning was specific to what the engine could represent at the time, not a permanent fact about signature weapons.
- Consequences: Migrating Red Hood off the `scheduled_nukes` approximation onto a real segment measurably moved her total damage (~+1.6% on a deck-buffed spot check) — the old number understated her whenever a deck actually buffed Charge Damage/ATK. Remaining weapon-transform work is deferred to a "plan 2" backlog (Cinderella: Crystal Wave's MG/Snipe dual-slug pair, `rapi-red-hood`'s Full-Burst-window-visible projectile launch, a verification pass on Snow White: Heavy Arms' charge-lock-on loop) — see `docs/engine-gaps.md` and the skill's `references/special-mechanics.md` ("Weapon-mode transforms") for the up-to-date list and per-item reasoning; not restated here.
## Phase 7 Stage 1 reconnaissance: cross-account roster sync IS feasible via a public ShiftyPad share URL — reverses the "public query impossible" working assumption
- Date: 2026-07-19
- Context: Phase 7's roadmap status going into this reconnaissance was one line — "requires login, public query impossible" (`docs/roadmap.md`) — an assumption that had never actually been tested against a second account. Verified against Fienn's own second blablalink account (account B, roster visibility set public via the shield toggle), queried from account A's already-logged-in Chrome session over the same API path the collector already uses (`tools/collect-blablalink/collect.js`'s `fetchOwned`/`collectDetails` — `GetUserCharacters`, `GetUserCharacterDetails`, `GetUserProfileOutpostInfo`).
- Decision (recorded as a finding, not a productization choice — see the open questions below): ShiftyPad's public share URL (`blablalink.com/shiftyspad?uid=<base64>`) decodes to `<shiftypad_region_id>-<intl_open_id>` (e.g. `29080-1234567890123456789`). From ANY authenticated session (not the target's own), calling the collector's existing three endpoints with the target's `open_id` and `nikke_area_id=81` (see the region-id gotcha in `docs/insights.md`) returns `code 0` and the full per-unit investment payload the B2 stat calculator (159/159, above) already consumes: `GetUserCharacters` (182 owned), `GetUserCharacterDetails` (`arm_equip_*`/`attractive_lv`/`harmony_cube_*`), `GetUserProfileOutpostInfo` (9 corporation-research rows). Combined, a consenting user's whole level-400 roster is computable with **zero per-unit page scraping**. An anonymous, session-less call is refused (`code 300001`, "game not login") — this is NOT a truly public API; it requires a valid CALLER-side blablalink session, but never the TARGET's credentials.
- Why: This changes Phase 7's shape from "build a per-user out-of-band ownership-proof flow" (the edenpj precedent — paste a code into the game profile bio) to "the user toggles roster visibility public and hands over their share URL" — a materially simpler consent mechanism, contingent on resolving who supplies the caller-side session (below).
- Consequences: Reverses the `docs/roadmap.md` Phase 7 "public query impossible" assumption (roadmap's Stage 1 entry already updated, 2026-07-19). Does **not** yet resolve who supplies the CALLER-side session for a hosted service — a service blablalink account would reintroduce an OPERATOR (not target) credential-storage question, which conflicts with the project's existing "never store credentials, tokens supplied by Fienn" posture (see "MVP user data = manual entry" below) unless explicitly re-decided. Open questions deferred to a future design pass, not decided here: (a) whether a PRIVATE (shield-off) account's data is actually gated on read, or just unlisted from search — only "public is readable" is confirmed, since account B was public; (b) the operator-session-storage question above; (c) how a cross-region user's `nikke_area_id` is discovered or defaulted — **resolved 2026-07-29**: blablalink's own `GetRegionList` is the authoritative list (81 Japan, 82 NA, 83 Korea, 84 Global, 85 SEA), and the app probes all five rather than guessing a default (see the two 2026-07-29 entries above on server probing and profile identity). Also observed in the same pass: edenpj.com (the OOB-proof precedent site) returned 502 during reconnaissance, but this was a **server-maintenance outage** (operator notice), not a shutdown, per Fienn — the OOB-proof pattern remains a valid reference even though the finding above makes that whole path unnecessary; nikkemimir.xyz survives but its `/sync` route is an unresolved SPA. The `resource_id`→slug map and B2 stat calculator need no changes regardless of which sync path is eventually chosen — blablalink's `open_id` is already the canonical per-account key either path would use. See `docs/insights.md` ("Data") for the region-id-vs-`nikke_area_id` gotcha this reconnaissance hit.

## Collector roster units map to slugs by `resource_id` in a dedicated table, with Favorite Item ownership kept in a SEPARATE set — identity and investment are different layers
- Date: 2026-07-18
- Context: this fixes the "game display name doesn't match the encoded slug" gap Phase B deferred (entry below). The collector's `roster.json` carries full directory names; the ExiaInvasion export carries short in-game names — and both went through ONE shared name-alias table, so the two import paths wanted contradictory mappings for the same string. Measured against Fienn's real roster: base "Soline" resolved to `soline-frost-ticket`, colliding with the true variant, and because drafts merge by `character_slug`, the variant's accurate collected stats were clobbered by the base's. Three distinct game units all display as "Rei" (831 레이 = `rei-ayanami`, 392 라이 = unencoded, 834 = tentative-name), which no name-based scheme can separate at all.
- Decision (Fienn, 2026-07-18): key the collector path by blablalink's `resource_id` — already present in `roster.json` — in a new authoritative table `frontend/src/lib/resourceIdSlugMap.ts`, fully decoupled from the name-alias table (which stays untouched for the ExiaInvasion path). That table maps `resource_id → **base** slug and never encodes user investment: ownership of a unit's in-game Favorite Item (애장품) lives in a separate `SIGNATURE_OWNED` set, with `DUAL_SLOT_BASES` naming the base slugs that have a `-signature` encoding. Full spec/plan: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-18-roster-resource-id-slug-map-design.md`, `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-18-roster-resource-id-slug-map.md`.
- Why the split (the non-obvious part): a "dual-slot" unit — both `<slug>` and `<slug>-signature` encoded, currently only Drake and Julia — has base and signature sharing ONE `resource_id` (Drake is 101 whether or not his Favorite Item is owned). No id-keyed table can distinguish them, because the discriminator isn't identity at all, it's per-user, per-point-in-time investment. Baking `101 → drake-signature` into the identity map would be wrong for every other user, and wrong for Fienn himself the moment his investment changes. Fienn also noted the set of Nikkes with Favorite Items keeps growing with game updates, so this needs one obvious place to update — which `SIGNATURE_OWNED` is, and which a future SSR-favorite auto-detection pass can populate mechanically instead of by hand. Alternatives rejected: (a) fix the shared alias table — impossible, the two paths want contradictory mappings for the same short name; (b) key by `name_code` (the earlier assumption recorded in the collector's RECIPE) — `resource_id` was already in `roster.json`, so it needs no extra collection; (c) bake the signature choice into the map — the fragility above; (d) auto-detect Favorite Item ownership now via the Collection tab's `favorite_rare` grade — deferred, since the collector doesn't capture that tab, the "signature = SSR grade" hypothesis couldn't be verified from local data, and it currently affects exactly one unit in Fienn's roster (Julia is uninvested).
- Consequences: unencoded owned units are deliberately KEPT as drafts (with a raw `deriveSlug` slug) rather than dropped — the backend already excludes non-encoded slugs before any simulation work, so keeping them costs nothing in accuracy or speed and preserves the user's visibility of units they own, including favourites we haven't encoded. A backend test (`backend/tests/test_resource_id_slug_map.py`) cross-checks the hand-authored frontend tables against the live `ENCODED_SLUGS`, which means **encoding any new Nikke now red-fails the backend suite until the frontend map or `KNOWN_UNMAPPED` is updated** — intentional, and documented in the `nikke-skill-encoding` skill. Residual gaps: `jill-valentine` has no `resource_id` (Fienn doesn't own her; never guessed), and the map is still hand-authored, so a wrong-but-valid id would not be caught — closing that needs a committed public directory snapshot, which requires a collector run.
## Per-unit data-file unification rejected for now; staged plan instead: drift detection → per-unit source migration → (only then) merge files
- Date: 2026-07-18
- Context: Fienn proposed merging each unit's two data files (`data/dotgg/char_*.json` + `data/lootandwaifus/char_*.json`) into one. Investigation showed the real problem is elsewhere: 22 skill-value manifests (21 units + drake-signature) still read runtime skill values from dotgg files, and dotgg froze ~2026-05 — so any balance patch to those units silently leaves the simulation on pre-patch numbers (proven on Scarlet: Black Shadow, see `insights.md`). A file merge wouldn't fix that: the two sources have structurally different skill-value representations (dotgg native `description_value_NN` slot dicts vs lootandwaifus per-level text with hand-tuned token numbering), so merged files either carry both representations (cosmetic) or force a 22-manifest migration anyway. Merging would also break `lootandwaifus_html_to_json.py`'s idempotent regenerate-from-HTML property.
- Decision (Fienn, 2026-07-18): reject the up-front file merge; adopt the staged plan — (1) build `scripts/check_skill_value_drift.py` (shipped: fetches live lootandwaifus for all 22 dotgg-source manifests, compares description-referenced dotgg slots against text tokens via sub-multiset containment, exit 1 on DRIFT); (2) migrate manifests to `source: "lootandwaifus"` per unit, prioritized by actual drift (run the script after game patches); (3) fold dotgg's remaining 6 weapon-stat fields into the lootandwaifus JSON only once no manifest reads dotgg skill values — at which point one-file-per-unit falls out nearly for free.
- Why: correctness before layout. The drift detector closes the live risk immediately (~250 lines), each migration is small and harness-verified, and the eventual merge becomes trivial instead of a 22-unit big-bang re-verification. First live run: 22 OK, 0 DRIFT — no unit is currently stale, so migration urgency is zero until the next balance patch.
- Consequences: run `python3 scripts/check_skill_value_drift.py` after NIKKE balance patches; any DRIFT slug is the next migration candidate. Two comparison caveats are documented in the script's docstrings: a stale value coincidentally equaling an unrelated token passes containment, and the referenced-slot filter assumes builders only consume description-referenced slots (verified true for all 22 as of 2026-07-18). Side effect of the first run: 18 units' lootandwaifus HTML/JSON newly collected — raw material for the migrations.

## Phase B collector ships: solo raid redefined to level 400, uninvested SSR included, PveCube level cap raised to 15, collector merge overwrites stats
- Date: 2026-07-18
- Context: the Phase B design (entry below) was approved but unimplemented; this entry records what actually shipped (`tools/collect-blablalink/{capture.js,parse.js,collect.js,RECIPE.md}`, `backend/app/models.py`, `frontend/src/lib/rosterImport.ts`, `frontend/src/types/nikkeDraft.ts`; commits `11f3f48..071a5d2`, 159/159 owned SSR captured end-to-end). Four sub-decisions had real alternatives:
  1. **Solo raid = level 400 fixed → `UserNikkeState.hp/atk/def` (`backend/app/models.py`) redefined to mean level-400 values; `actual_hp/actual_atk/actual_def` (all optional) added for the real-level number, for a future Union Raid mode.** Solo raid content normalizes every account to character level 400; the prior manual-entry convention used real level (e.g. 663), which measured ~2.9x too high (Rapi: 418,862 actual vs 143,543 @400) — every Solo Raid recommendation made before this shipped was ranking on inflated ATK. The engine itself needed no change — it just consumes whatever's in `hp/atk/def`.
  2. **`PveCube.level` cap raised from `le=10` to `le=15`** (backend model + frontend `CONSTRAINTS` mirror) — real cubes reach LV.15, and the stale `le=10` rejected valid imports. Chosen over clamping (which would silently lose data) per Fienn. Cube level itself is engine-inert today (flat cube stats already fold into ShiftyPad's displayed ATK — see the cube-double-count entry below; there's no name+level→effect% wiring yet), so only the validation cap mattered practically.
  3. **Collect ALL owned SSR, including uninvested (level-1) units, not just invested ones.** The 159-unit E2E collection is 135 invested (lv400+) + 24 uninvested (lv1, including meta units like 2B and Asuka). Because solo raid normalizes to 400 regardless of starting level, capturing an uninvested unit is just as valid as an invested one (the collector steps the level slider bidirectionally — up from lv1 or down from a real level — and `parse.js` accepts a positive delta for the up-stepped case, not just the negative delta an invested unit shows). Chosen over skipping uninvested units (simpler, but drops real roster data and would silently exclude a unit the moment the user invests in it later) per Fienn. The recommendation engine naturally won't pick a lv1 unit's inflated-by-nothing stats, so this costs nothing at query time.
  4. **A collector-specific roster merge (`mergeCollectorDrafts` in `frontend/src/hooks/useRoster.ts`) overwrites stats on import, unlike the Phase A ExiaInvasion merge (`mergeRosterDrafts`), which preserves them.** The ExiaInvasion export carries no stats at all (see Phase A entry below), so its merge preserves any hand-entered ATK/cube. The collector's `roster.json` IS the stats source of truth, so on an existing unit it overwrites stats/cube/actual-level fields, preserving only `id` and `core_level`. Two different merge functions exist because the two importers have opposite trust relationships to the roster's stat fields — collapsing them into one merge would either wipe hand-entered ExiaInvasion-only data or stale-lock collector data.
- Why: each of these directly fixes a measured correctness gap (level-400 skew, cube-level rejection, silently-missing uninvested units, wrong merge trust) rather than being speculative scope.
- Consequences: every Solo Raid recommendation made against the old manually-entered actual-level stats is now known-wrong and should be treated as stale until re-collected via the Phase B pipeline. `docs/roadmap.md` marks Phase B complete. See the companion insights in `docs/insights.md` ("Data") for the scraping/DOM techniques that made this possible, and the name→slug mapping gap that still silently excludes a handful of units from recommendation post-import (`resolveSlug`, deferred).

## Phase B redefinition: an accurate blablalink/ShiftyPad live stat collector — read ShiftyPad's already-computed values, don't reimplement the stat formula (design approved, not yet implemented)
- Date: 2026-07-18
- Context: Phase A (below) left ATK/HP/DEF and per-character cube manual. Investigating blablalink CDN endpoints found the raw ingredients to compute final ATK (base-stat-by-level arrays, breakthrough/core coefficients) but also found a much simpler alternative already exists: ShiftyPad's individual-Nikke page (`blablalink.com/shiftyspad/nikke?nikke=<resource_id>`) computes and displays the fully-assembled stat (armor included) client-side, plus a level slider that can retarget the display to any level including 400.
- Decision (Fienn, 2026-07-18): (1) Build a Playwright/CDP live collector (a personal tool, not backend/frontend code) that attaches to Fienn's already-logged-in browser and scrapes ShiftyPad directly — stats, overload (from the same page's "Equipment Effects," already summed across 4 gear slots), skills, collectibles, and cube (from the page's Cube/Collection/Skill tabs) — rather than reimplementing blablalink's stat formula from the raw CDN tables. Reasoning given: ShiftyPad is an official sub-property of blablalink, so it's a stable target, and reading its output sidesteps deriving the armor stat table and the breakthrough-% formula by hand. Fallback for anything the ShiftyPad DOM can't reliably deliver (the Skill tab's selector is currently unresolved) is the authenticated raw API (`GetUserCharacterDetails`); fallback for everything is manual entry / the Phase A file importer, per the project's "third parties die" posture. (2) Collect stats in **two versions per character**: actual-level (for a future Union Raid mode) and level-400 (for Solo Raid). (3) **Redefine Solo Raid to level 400** — Solo Raid content is confirmed level-locked, so `UserNikkeState.hp/atk/def` (`backend/app/models.py`) are redefined to mean level-400 stats (no engine change needed — the engine just consumes whatever's in those fields), with new optional `actual_hp/atk/def` fields added for the future Union Raid path.
- Why: reading ShiftyPad's computed output avoids re-deriving and maintaining a stat formula (armor stat tables, breakthrough-% curve) that blablalink itself could change without notice, in exchange for depending on ShiftyPad's DOM staying scrapeable (mitigated by falling back to the raw API, then to manual entry). The level-400 redefinition corrects a real accuracy bug: the deck-builder currently uses manually-entered actual-level (663) ATK for Solo Raid recommendations, which is 2.9x too high (Rapi measured: 418,862 actual vs 143,543 at level 400) — every Solo Raid recommendation made so far has been ranking on inflated numbers.
- Consequences: not implemented as of this entry — design approved (`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-18-blablalink-stat-collector-design.md`) and a plan exists (`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-18-blablalink-stat-collector.md`), but the collector, the model field changes, and the accuracy-correcting resync have not shipped. Once it lands, every Solo Raid recommendation made against manually-entered actual-level stats should be treated as suspect until re-collected at level 400. `cookie`/session credentials are never persisted by the collector (same posture as Phase A, below) — it rides Fienn's own already-authenticated browser session via CDP, not stored tokens. See the companion insights in `docs/insights.md` ("Data") on the class-uniform base-stat finding this design leans on.

## Phase A: ExiaInvasion JSON roster import lands in the frontend, merging manual ATK/cube fields by slug rather than replacing the whole roster
- Date: 2026-07-18
- Context: Fienn's stated pain (Phase 7 investigation) was the ~2,000-value first-fill roster survey and needing to redo it on every gear upgrade. The open-source ExiaInvasion browser extension exports a user's blablalink data as JSON (skills, breakthrough, synchro level, per-slot overload rolls), but the export contains no final ATK/HP/DEF and no per-character equipped cube — both need to stay manual until Phase B. Alternatives for the importer's home: backend Python (would need a network/session story) vs. frontend TS (pure local file parsing, no auth).
- Decision (Fienn, 2026-07-18): build the importer as a pure frontend TS parser (`frontend/src/lib/exiaImport.ts`). It maps one ExiaInvasion export into `NikkeDraft[]`: skill levels, breakthrough (`limit_break.core`→`core_level`), synchro level (`synchroLevel`→`level`, inert — not consumed by any skill_rule/base_stats path), and overload — 4 equipment slots' `function_type` rolls summed per type, then mapped to the backend's Korean 7-name overload vocabulary (`overload_effects.py`'s `NAME_TO_STAT`); unmapped types (`StatDef`, `StatAccuracyCircle` — common, ~55 rows in the real export) are dropped with a user-visible warning, not an error, since erroring would make import fail almost every time. `useRoster.importDrafts` merges the incoming drafts into the existing roster **by `character_slug`**: import fields (skills/overload/breakthrough/level) are overwritten, but `hp`/`atk`/`def_`/`hasCube`/`pve_cube` and the existing draft `id` are preserved. `cookie` and `game_uid` (both present at the export's top level — session credentials and account ID) are never read or stored by the parser.
- Why: the export has neither final ATK/HP/DEF (blablalink computes those client-side in ShiftyPad, not present in the export) nor per-character equipped cube (the export's `cubes` key is only the account-wide cube inventory) — so both remain genuinely manual inputs, not an oversight. Slug-by-slug merge (not whole-roster replace) exists specifically because the roster persists in localStorage and re-importing after every gear change would otherwise wipe hand-entered ATK/cube every time, reproducing the exact "re-enter everything" pain the importer was built to fix.
- Consequences: `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-07-18-exia-roster-importer-design.md`/`docs/superpowers/plans/2026-07-18-exia-roster-importer.md` hold the full field-mapping/alias tables (9 slug aliases, e.g. `ada`→`ada-wong`, derived from a 75-character export sample; 7-name overload mapping). Merged into `wip/scaffolding` — see commits `58e2ef2`..`ddbd7ab`, `a17eebe` ("Phase A ExiaInvasion importer complete" in `docs/roadmap.md`). ATK/cube automation is deferred to Phase B (entry above) rather than attempted here — this importer alone does not change what the deck-builder's ATK numbers mean, only how tediously they're re-typed.

## ShiftyPad's displayed hp/atk/def already include the equipped cube; cube stats are never re-added on top
- Date: 2026-07-17
- Context: `UserNikkeState.hp/atk/def` (`backend/app/models.py`) are the numbers a user copies from ShiftyPad, and `cube_effects.py::cube_to_effects` separately converts the equipped cube's own stats into Effects — an open question (flagged in `models.py` since the form was built) was whether ShiftyPad's displayed hp/atk/def already fold in the equipped cube, or show bare character stats with the cube meant to be added on top. Overload options were the nearby precedent, and they resolve the opposite way: Fienn had already confirmed ShiftyPad shows overload values SEPARATELY from the character-info stats, so they ARE additive (`overload_effects.py`) — which made cube_effects' old "Like overload options" docstring opener actively misleading, inviting exactly the wrong inference for cubes.
- Decision (Fienn, 2026-07-17): with a cube equipped, ShiftyPad's displayed hp/atk/def already reflect it; with none equipped, it shows bare character stats. Cube stats must therefore never be re-added on top of `UserNikkeState.hp/atk/def`.
- Why: this is a fact about what the data source shows, not a modeling choice — but it directly determines whether `cube_to_effects` may ever map a cube stat onto `atk`/`def_`/`max_hp` (it must not, or the simulation double-counts the cube's ATK/DEF/HP contribution once via the character stats and again via an Effect).
- Consequences: no behavior change was needed — `cube_to_effects` only ever emitted `reload_speed_percent` and `other_elemental_bonus` (superior code damage), neither of which is part of hp/atk/def, so the existing mapping was already correct, now for a stated reason rather than by accident. The guard going forward: a cube must never contribute `atk`/`def`/`max_hp` in `cube_effects.py`, or it double-counts. `models.py` and `cube_effects.py`'s docstrings now state this explicitly so the question doesn't reopen. See the companion insight in `docs/insights.md` ("Data") on why this matters far more for automated ingestion than manual entry.
